xmlns=\"uri\" (default) or xmlns:hp=\"uri\"\n    for m in re.finditer(r'\\sxmlns(?::(?P<prefix>[A-Za-z_][\\w\\-.]*))?=[\\\"\\'](?P<uri>[^\\\"\\']+)[\\\"\\']', xml_text):\n        prefix = m.group('prefix') or ''\n        uri = m.group('uri')\n        ET.register_namespace(prefix, uri)\n\n\ndef normalize_budget(v: str) -> str:\n    # Keep leading currency symbol (₩), remove commas in the numeric part.\n    if not isinstance(v, str):\n        v = str(v)\n    if v.startswith('₩'):\n        return '₩' + v[1:].replace(',', '')\n    return v.replace(',', '')\n\n\ndef replace_placeholders(text: str, data: dict) -> tuple[str, bool]:\n    missing = set()\n\n    def repl(m: re.Match) -> str:\n        key = m.group(1).strip()\n        if key not in data:\n            missing.add(key)\n            return m.group(0)\n        val = data[key]\n        if key == '예산':\n            return normalize_budget(val)\n        return str(val)\n\n    new_text = PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(repl, text)\n    if missing:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Missing placeholder keys in JSON: {sorted(missing)}\")\n    return new_text, (new_text != text)\n\n\nRANGE_RE = re.compile(r'(\\d{4})[.\\-](\\d{1,2})\\s*~\\s*(\\d{4})[.\\-](\\d{1,2})')\nSINGLE_RE = re.compile(r'(\\d{4})[.\\-](\\d{1,2})')\n\n\ndef month_span_from_text(text: str) -> int | None:\n    \"\"\"Extract a YYYY-MM ~ YYYY-MM range or a single YYYY-MM and return inclusive month count.\"\"\"\n    m = RANGE_RE.search(text)\n    if m:\n        sy, sm, ey, em = map(int, m.groups())\n        diff = (ey - sy) * 12 + (em - sm)\n        if diff < 0:\n            return None\n        return diff + 1  # inclusive\n\n    # Single month like (2027-01)\n    ms = SINGLE_RE.findall(text)\n    if not ms:\n        return None\n\n    # If the text contains a range, RANGE_RE would have matched earlier.\n    # After placeholder substitution, append month span on phase lines.\n        # This should apply to lines like \"1단계: ... (2026-08 ~ 2026-10)\".\n        new_text2, did2 = append_month_span_if_phase_line(new_text)\n        new_text = new_text2\n        modified = modified or did2\n\n        if modified:\n            collapse_paragraph_runs(p, hp_ns, new_text)\n            remove_linesegarray(p, hp_ns)\n            changed_any = True\n\n    if changed_any:\n        tree.write(xml_path, encoding='utf-8', xml_declaration=True)\n\n    return changed_any\n\n\ndef rezip_hwpx(work_dir: str, out_path: str) -> None:\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(out_path, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:\n        for root, dirs, files in os.walk(work_dir):\n            for f in files:\n                full = os.path.join(root, f)\n                arcname = os.path.relpath(full, work_dir)\n                zf.write(full, arcname)\n\n\ndef main():\n    data = load_data(JSON_PATH)\n\n    contents_dir = os.path.join(WORK_DIR, 'Contents')\n    xml_files = []\n    for name in os.listdir(contents_dir):\n        if name.lower().endswith('.xml'):\n            xml_files.append(os.path.join(contents_dir, name))\n\n    if not xml_files:\n        raise RuntimeError(f'No XML files found under {contents_dir}')\n\n    changed = []\n    for xp in sorted(xml_files):\n        if process_section_xml(xp, data):\n            changed.append(xp)\n\n    if not changed:\n        raise RuntimeError('No changes were applied (no placeholders/phase lines found?).')\n\n    rezip_hwpx(WORK_DIR, OUT_PATH)\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"}}],"model":"openai/gpt-5.2-20251211","stop_reason":null,"stop_details":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":null,"cache_read_input_tokens":null,"cache_creation":null,"inference_geo":null,"server_tool_use":null,"service_tier":null,"speed":"standard"},"provider":"OpenAI","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ff155d81-82b6-4b72-9033-175c524b6367","uuid":"43cd1ae3-bd66-4e6a-8feb-5617a13029a4"}
